The mode identity stops at the chip + card border — the *room* never changes color.\n\n| # | Gap | Spec | Where |\n|---|-----|------|-------|\n| A1 | **Safari blur dead** | `backdrop-filter` at 3 sites has no `-webkit-` twin → iOS Safari renders flat panels. Add `-webkit-backdrop-filter` beside each. **MANDATORY** (portal.css doctrine). | html:36, 112, ~156 |\n| A2 | **No reduced-motion guard** | Wrap `pulse`/`bounce`/`fadeIn`/`shimmer` in `@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce){ animation:none }`. Accessibility + polish. | html `<style>` tail |\n| A3 | **No responsive breakpoints** | `@media (max-width:480px)`: landing cards stack 1-col, header h1 font 14px, mic 64px. `@media (min-width:1024px)`: center feed in a max-width 760px column so it's not a full-width void on desktop/Mac. | html `<style>` tail |\n| A4 | **Mode never dresses the room** | The whole point of 3 modes. On mode change, set a `data-mode` attr on `<body>` and drive: header border tint, mic button color, `#dot.ok` accent, feed bubble-obi border — all off the mode color. Today `#mic` is hardcoded purple. Phonics session should *feel* teal end-to-end. | css + `selectMode`/`confirmLandingMode` in chat.js |\n| A5 | **Lion lost its ring** | `.obi-avatar` was a gradient orb with a pulsing ring; now it's a bare `<img>`. Re-add the live ring as an animated `box-shadow` on the container so Obi looks *alive*, mode-tinted. | html:199-208 |\n| A6 | **Landing is purple-only** | When a card is selected, bloom the `Start Session →` button + heading to that mode's color. Currently always purple regardless of choice. | html:228/254 + `selectLandingMode` |\n\n---\n\n## PART B — FUNCTIONALITY HARDENING (the real risk surface)\n\nThis is where the audit found things that **bite at scale**, ranked by blast radius:\n\n### 🔴 B1 — SECURITY: raw Deepgram account key shipped to every browser `[BB-43]`\n`index.js:866` → `const access_token = apiKey;` then returned as `token` (line 919). The comment claims \"raw key exposure here is acceptable\" because `/v1/auth/grant` with `asr:write` returned 401. **It is not acceptable** — this is the long-lived master account key. Any student opening devtools copies it and can burn unlimited usage on Michael's Deepgram account.\n- **Root cause of the 401:** wrong scope. Voice Agent WS (`/v1/agent/converse`) needs an agent-converse scope, not `asr:write`. The grant flow was abandoned against the wrong scope.\n- **Fix path 1 (preferred):** correct the grant request scope → mint a 60s ephemeral token per session. Key never leaves the server long-lived.\n- **Fix path 2 (fallback):** proxy the WS through the worker (key fully server-side; costs latency + a Durable-Object/WS relay).\n- **This is the #1 item.** It's a credential leak, not a polish gap.\n\n### 🟠 B2 — No reconnect backoff / circuit breaker\n`chat.js:851` → fixed `setTimeout(…, 2000)` on every drop, **infinite, no cap**. If Deepgram blips or the token endpoint 500s, every client hammers `/api/deepgram-token` every 2s forever — and each call does **6 KV reads + a full prompt build** (index.js:856-863). At scale, a Deepgram outage becomes a self-inflicted KV-read storm on your own worker.\n- **Spec:** exponential backoff `2s→4s→8s→16s→cap 30s`, max 5 auto-attempts, then stop and show `Tap to reconnect` (manual). Reset counter on a clean `SettingsApplied`.\n\n### 🟠 B3 — No think/speak fallback → silent session death\n`index.js:900-911` — single `think` provider (`gpt-4o-mini`), single `speak` voice (`aura-2-zeus-en`), no fallback arrays. `FAILED_TO_THINK` / `FAILED_TO_SPEAK` = the lesson dies mid-sentence with no recovery. Deepgram supports fallback providers (v4.9.0+).\n- **Spec:** add a secondary think model + secondary speak voice to Settings so one provider blip doesn't end class.\n\n### 🟡 B4 — No prompt-length guard (25K cap)\n`index.js:902` inserts `buildAgentPrompt(...)` raw. A student with rich memory+vocab+arc can exceed Deepgram's **25,000-char managed-LLM cap → `PROMPT_TOO_LONG`** truncates instructions mid-rule. Clamp/trim to ~24K with the system rules pinned first.\n\n### 🟡 B5 — `visibilitychange` listener leak\n`chat.js:1191` adds a new `visibilitychange` listener **every** `startAgentListening`, no `{once}`, never removed. Reconnect N times → N listeners all firing `resumeCtx`. Hoist to one module-level listener.\n\n### 🟡 B6 — iOS mic reconnect after drop `[Open Loop #4]`\n`chat.js:851-859` auto-reacquires `getUserMedia` inside the 2s timeout — **outside a user gesture**, which iOS Safari silently denies. The `pendingMicStream` path can't save it on iOS.\n- **Spec:** detect iOS; on drop, *don't* auto-reacquire — surface an explicit `Tap to resume` and call `getUserMedia` **inside** that tap.\n\n### ⚪ B7 — Comment/value drift\n`chat.js:1032` says \"within 4s\"; timer fires at `2500ms` (line 1042). Align — small, but it's the drift that misleads next session.\n\n---\n\n## DISPATCH PLAN (on switch back to Sonnet + \"go\")\n\n| Chunk | Items | Route | Why |\n|-------|-------|-------|-----|\n| **C1 — Facelift** | A1–A6 | **Inline (Sonnet)** | CSS/DOM edits needing live file state; PA has no FS access (run055 P1) |\n| **C2 — Client hardening** | B2, B5, B6, B7 | **Inline (Sonnet)** | Surgical `chat.js` edits, live state |\n| **C3 — Server hardening** | B3, B4 | **PA T1** | Self-contained Settings-object edits, content passable in context |\n| **C4 — BB-43 security** | B1 | **PA T2 (Opus)** | Irreversible auth/security decision — grant-scope vs WS-proxy is a doctrine call, T2 per L43 |\n\n**Gate:** C1+C2 → DEV deploy → browser verify → only then prod.
